# Starter coverage: research priorities

Policy agreed 2026-11-09: new players of every playable race and faction should find nearby completed Buddies. This is an interim coverage milestone inside the wider NPC goal, not a reduction of that goal.

The completed-profile audit is in [npc-coverage.json](../npc-coverage.json). NPC race, friendly-faction evidence and starting-area access are separate measures. Public definitions can be sparse: 66 completed profiles lack a faction icon in the current node, so those are marked unverified rather than guessed from NPC race.

## First Horde research shortlist

| Priority | NPC | ID | Intended route |
| --- | --- | ---: | --- |
| 1 | Master Gadrin | 3188 | Durotar / troll and orc early route |
| 2 | Chief Hawkwind | 2981 | Mulgore / Tauren |
| 3 | Mai'ah | 5884 | Durotar / troll and orc early route |
| 4 | Hana'zua | 3287 | Durotar / troll and orc early route |
| 5 | Zureetha Fargaze | 3145 | Durotar / troll and orc early route |

All five exact names were checked against current Forever NPC nodes. Their character models resolve through the cached DB2 display/extra tables; Gadrin, Hawkwind and Mai'ah now have completed profiles and four v4 recordings each; see [their research notes](horde-starter-trio.md). Hana'zua and Zureetha now also have completed profiles and four recordings each; see [their research notes](valley-trials-hunter-and-guide.md). The next rotation moves to the thinner Mulgore selection.

Gadrin's current node explicitly reports Horde-friendly reaction and a Durotar pin. The other four current definitions confirm identity/race but omit reaction/location fields; current quest/dialogue or equivalent current client evidence must fill those gaps before authoring. They are not marked friendly merely because they are orcs, trolls or Tauren. Greatmother Hawkwind (2991), Chief Hawkwind's mother, is identified in the primary quest text. Her current node returned 404; current availability still needs review before she is added to the shortlist. The missing node does not establish death or removal.

Four of these candidates are absent from the original filtered public catalog. This shows why that snapshot is not an exhaustive census of eligible Horde NPCs. They are retained through the explicit research-priority source with uncertainty visible, rather than excluded as generic or silently treated as removed. No account/character progress is changed by this authoring queue.

## Coverage baseline and rotation

Aim first for at least five fully completed named adult NPC profiles accessible within each starting experience: Elwynn, Dun Morogh, Teldrassil, Durotar, Tirisfal, Mulgore, and both faction paths on Zephras Isle. This is a target, not a claim those thresholds are already met. Include a useful mixture of quest givers, trainers, merchants/services and named residents; cover both sexes where verified candidates exist. Shared dwarf/gnome and orc/troll starts should include NPCs of both races. Every profile keeps the same lore, approximately fifteen personal lines, and 3-5 signature-recording standard.

Horde starter batches take priority while the imbalance is large. Alternate under-covered routes rather than completing another long run of Stormwind or Lakeshire profiles. Night elf, dwarf/gnome and Skyborne starter coverage remains in rotation so correcting one faction gap does not reproduce the same race/zone gap elsewhere. Track neutral NPCs separately; do not use them to claim faction-specific access without verification. Capitals and high-level destinations do not substitute for a beginner's first few Buddies.

[Blizzard's race overview](https://worldofwarcraft.blizzard.com/en-us/news/24304075/) establishes Alliance High Order and Horde Windshaper Skyborne; [the launch overview](https://news.blizzard.com/en-us/article/24315820/world-of-warcraft-forever-now-live) identifies the Zephras Isle starting experience. Both paths need access checks rather than assuming the same friendly NPCs are available on each.

No race, sex, adult model or friendly-access decision is inferred from a name or role alone.

## Zephras coverage underway

### Next CurseForge release milestone

User authorized publishing to CurseForge when the addon reaches a good release point (2026-11-09). The user clarified that the next publication is v002; v001 is the prior release. Use the initial five-completed-Buddies-per-starting-experience milestone, including verified access for both Skyborne paths, as that checkpoint. Preserve full lore and audio standards; do not count pending candidates or infer faction access from race. After reaching it, review the project description and v002 changelog, run the release checks, refresh and verify the v002 ZIP, and publish it to the existing BuddyDex CurseForge project. Confirm the actual project and account through the available authenticated service before uploading; no CurseForge project ID or upload integration is currently recorded in the repository. Publication authorization does not imply that the wider NPC-profile goal is finished.

Nyalah Brightfire is complete with fifteen personal lines and four female Skyborne v4 signatures; see [her research](nyalah-brightfire.md). Current explicit friendly icons and both-faction quest access support one shared profile for either starter path. This remains one unique NPC, and both five-profile milestones are still open. The audit now has 272 unique profiles: 187 Alliance-access listings, 27 Horde-access listings and 59 unverified; the shared listing makes faction access totals overlap.

The [Zephras research queue](zephras-first-candidates.md) and machine-readable priorities now include early guides, a Windshaper guide, an innkeeper and faction-specific shaman trainers. A sparse public quest-tab listing is not taken as proof of no chain. Complete the full linked quests before authoring.

Ailee Farheart and Rorian the Dayseeker are complete for v002; see [their full chain research](thendal-first-guides.md). Zephras now has three unique shared profiles accessible to both faction paths: Nyalah, Ailee and Rorian. This includes a male Windshaper and female High Order NPCs, but both five-profile milestones remain open. The audit now contains 274 unique completed profiles, with overlapping public access totals of 189 Alliance and 29 Horde plus 59 unverified. Continue with a service NPC and the remaining faction-specific trainer/quest research rather than returning to Alliance capitals.
